  What does this sentence mean?
  Stette con suo padre.
   You win with number seven.  |   Do you think I'm ill?  |  *She stayed with her father.  |   I haven't spoken to Tom in three weeks.

[multiple_choice] it:fare#form:passato_remoto  (skill passato-remoto-irregular)
  What does this sentence mean?
  Io e Tom lo facemmo.
   I am taking tomorrow afternoon off.  |  *Tom and I did that.  |   I guess we'll never know now.  |   I'm sure Tom knew that.

[multiple_choice] it:volere#form:passato_remoto  (skill passato-remoto-irregular)
  What does this sentence mean?
  Disse che non volle mai farlo.
   He took a picture of the beautiful landscape.  |   Look what I made for her.  |   I can't make her happy.  |  *She said she never wanted to do that.

[multiple_choice] it:dire#form:passato_remoto  (skill passato-remoto-irregular)
  What does this sentence mean?
  Dissero che persero la loro chiave.
   I don't think Tom would deny it.  |   Only then did we learn the truth.  |   Do you want me to tell you a story?  |  *They said that they lost their key.

[multiple_choice] it:sapere#form:passato_remoto  (skill passato-remoto-irregular)
  What does this sentence mean?
  Tom non seppe mai che Mary era ricca.
   With this coat I won't feel the cold.  |  *Tom never knew Mary was rich.  |   We know who our enemies are.  |   I don't like liars.

[multiple_choice] it:vedere#form:passato_remoto  (skill passato-remoto-irregular)
  What does this sentence mean?
  Dissero che non la videro mai.
   I'm not saying this to make myself likeable. I mean it.  |  *They said they never saw her.  |   Don't get excited. It's nothing.  |   You don't have to wait until the end.

========================================================================
SKILL relatives (grammar)  In cui, il quale: relative pronouns
========================================================================
--- flashcard: 8 example(s)
[flashcard] it:cui.2#prod  (skill relatives)
  How do you say this?
  which, whom, whose  =>  cui   [pron]

[flashcard] it:cui.2#rec  (skill relatives)
  What does this mean?
  cui  =>  which, whom, whose   [pron]

[flashcard] it:ciò#prod  (skill relatives)
  How do you say this?
  that, this, what  =>  ciò   [pron]

[flashcard] it:ciò#rec  (skill relatives)
  What does this mean?
  ciò  =>  that, this, what   [pron]

[flashcard] it:chiunque#prod  (skill relatives)
  How do you say this?
  anyone, whoever  =>  chiunque   [pron]

[flashcard] it:chiunque#rec  (skill relatives)
  What does this mean?
  chiunque  =>  anyone, whoever   [pron]

[flashcard] it:qualunque.2#prod  (skill relatives)
  How do you say this?
  any, whatever  =>  qualunque   [adj]

[flashcard] it:qualunque.2#rec  (skill relatives)
  What does this mean?
  qualunque  =>  any, whatever   [adj]

--- multiple_choice: 8 example(s)
[multiple_choice] it:cui.2#prod  (skill relatives)
  Pick the right word
  which
   noi  |  *cui  |   chiunque  |   ciò

[multiple_choice] it:cui.2#rec  (skill relatives)
  Pick the right translation
  cui
   you  |   anyone  |  *which  |   everything

[multiple_choice] it:ciò#prod  (skill relatives)
  Pick the right word
  that
   chiunque  |   cui  |   io  |  *ciò

[multiple_choice] it:ciò#rec  (skill relatives)
  Pick the right translation
  ciò
  *that  |   something  |   anyone  |   oneself

[multiple_choice] it:chiunque#prod  (skill relatives)
  Pick the right word
  anyone
   lo  |   ciò  |   cui  |  *chiunque

[multiple_choice] it:chiunque#rec  (skill relatives)
  Pick the right translation
  chiunque
  *anyone  |   which  |   that  |   she

[multiple_choice] it:qualunque.2#prod  (skill relatives)
  Pick the right word
  any
  *qualunque  |   giovane  |   pieno  |   contento

[multiple_choice] it:qualunque.2#rec  (skill relatives)
  Pick the right translation
  qualunque
  *any  |   black  |   young  |   different

  ciò  =  that
  qualunque  =  any
  dovunque  =  wherever
  chiunque  =  anyone
  sì  =  yes

[match_pairs] it:chiunque#rec  (skill relatives)
  Match the pairs
  signora  =  lady
  cui  =  which
  chiunque  =  anyone
  dovunque  =  wherever
  qualunque  =  any

--- sentence_builder: 8 example(s)

  tiles: non · hanno · cui · parlare · amici · ha · hai · con · avrebbe
  => non ha amici con cui parlare
